# If RBI injects cash, how much do banks move, and when?

GIR study, 21 Nov 2026 14:37 IST. Desk order 21 Nov 2026 11:45. Pure code, no AI calls, research only - a base rate, NOT PROVEN, not a trading rule.
Script: /home/globalbot/intraday/v2/kb/rbi_liquidity_study.py. Raw pages: /home/globalbot/girdata/ext/rbi_liquidity_study/raw/ (RBI press-release month lists + every liquidity release, saved untouched, read as data only; Yahoo daily bars).

## Plain words first

**Short answer: mostly no.** Most horizons look like a normal day; the exceptions (vs the same clock time on other days) are: NIFTY PSU BANK +30 min: up-rate +9 pts, median +0.05 pts.
- NIFTY BANK: auction close -> day's close up 52% (median +0.02%) vs 50% (median -0.00%) normally; whole day up 49% of injection days vs 53% of all days; next session up 50% vs 53%.
- NIFTY PSU BANK: auction close -> day's close up 46% (median -0.06%) vs 45% (median -0.10%) normally; whole day up 52% of injection days vs 52% of all days; next session up 55% vs 52%.
- NIFTY PVT BANK: auction close -> day's close up 51% (median +0.01%) vs 49% (median -0.02%) normally; whole day up 49% of injection days vs 53% of all days; next session up 50% vs 53%.
- How fast: there is no 'fast' move to time. BANKNIFTY median from the auction close: +30 min -0.01%, +60 min -0.01%, close +0.02%, next close -0.02%. The best / worst 30-min moves (+0.66 / -0.70) come from the day's other news, not from the auction.
- Size does not change it: BANKNIFTY auction close -> close, < 25k cr 50% up, med +0.01% (n 101); 25-75k 53% up, med +0.05% (n 137); > 75k 52% up, med +0.01% (n 87).
- Type: routine VRR days - whole day 50% up, med +0.01% (n 286); structural days (OMO buy, FX buy/sell swap, LTRO/TLTRO, CRR cut) - whole day 47% up, med -0.07% (n 57), next session 46% up, med -0.09% (n 57). Structural days are, if anything, weaker: RBI makes big structural injections in stress spells (Mar-Apr 2020 is most of the LTRO/TLTRO rows), so those days carry the stress rather than a cure for it.
- Tight auctions (bids >= 2x the amount) are no better: BANKNIFTY auction close -> close 45% up, med -0.03% (n 58) vs 53% up, med +0.04% (n 267) when cover < 2x.
- System liquidity the day before (RBI's daily Money Market Operations table): injections into a system already in SURPLUS (as on 20 Nov 2026) - BANKNIFTY auction close -> close 56% up, med +0.09% (n 160); into a DEFICIT - 48% up, med -0.02% (n 164). A small tilt, not a rule.
- The one pattern worth watching - switch days (RBI flips from absorbing to injecting after 3+ absorb-only operation days, as on 20 Nov 2026): BANKNIFTY auction close -> close 81% up, med +0.47% (n 16) (without 20 Nov: 79% up, med +0.39% (n 14)), whole day 65% up, med +0.42% (n 23). It holds with a looser / stricter definition (2+ days: 82% up, med +0.58% (n 17); 5+ days: 82% up, med +0.36% (n 11)). NIFTY on the same days: 81% up, med +0.32% (n 16) - so it is a whole-market tilt, not a bank-only one. Small n; chance check p = 0.057 before allowing for the ~30 splits tried - NOT PROVEN.

**Question:** if RBI injects cash, how much do bank indices move, and when? **Data:** 386 RBI injection operations on 324 days (2020-01-01 -> 2026-11-20) read from RBI's own press releases, plus 400 absorptions as a contrast. 325 injections fall on days with minute data (Nov 2023 ->), measured from the moment the auction window closed (RBI's pages do not show the result time) - or from the real result time where GIR's own feed recorded it (20 Nov 2026 only).
- **NIFTY BANK** after an injection: +30 min 47% up, med -0.01% (n 325); +60 min 47% up, med -0.01% (n 322); to the close 52% up, med +0.02% (n 325); to the next close 49% up, med -0.02% (n 323). Normal for the same clock time on other days: +30 min 50% up / med +0.00%, close 50% up / med -0.00%. Whole day (prev close -> close): 49% up, med -0.01% (n 323) vs all days 53% up / med +0.08%.
- **NIFTY PSU BANK** after an injection: +30 min 55% up, med +0.03% (n 325); +60 min 52% up, med +0.03% (n 322); to the close 46% up, med -0.06% (n 325); to the next close 50% up, med -0.01% (n 323). Normal for the same clock time on other days: +30 min 46% up / med -0.02%, close 45% up / med -0.10%. Whole day (prev close -> close): 52% up, med +0.07% (n 322) vs all days 52% up / med +0.08%.
- **NIFTY PVT BANK** after an injection: +30 min 49% up, med -0.00% (n 325); +60 min 49% up, med -0.01% (n 322); to the close 51% up, med +0.01% (n 325); to the next close 53% up, med +0.05% (n 323). Normal for the same clock time on other days: +30 min 51% up / med +0.00%, close 49% up / med -0.02%. Whole day (prev close -> close): 49% up, med -0.02% (n 323) vs all days 53% up / med +0.06%.
- **Size** (BANKNIFTY, window close -> close / whole day): < 25k cr: 50% up, med +0.01% (n 101) / 55% up, med +0.08% (n 121); 25-75k cr: 53% up, med +0.05% (n 137) / 45% up, med -0.11% (n 148); > 75k cr: 52% up, med +0.01% (n 87) / 49% up, med -0.01% (n 92).
- **Type** (BANKNIFTY whole day / next session): routine VRR: 50% up, med +0.01% (n 286) / 50% up, med +0.00% (n 286); structural: 47% up, med -0.07% (n 57) / 46% up, med -0.09% (n 57); OMO purchase: 46% up, med -0.07% (n 37) / 54% up, med +0.03% (n 37); FX buy/sell swap: 57% up, med +0.22% (n 7) / 14% up, med -0.29% (n 7); CRR cut: 75% up, med +1.04% (n 4) / 50% up, med +0.14% (n 4); LTRO/TLTRO: 40% up, med -0.73% (n 10) / 30% up, med -0.58% (n 10).
- **Bid-cover** (BANKNIFTY window close -> close / whole day): tight >= 2x 45% up, med -0.03% (n 58) / 48% up, med -0.03% (n 77); < 2x 53% up, med +0.04% (n 267) / 50% up, med +0.01% (n 266).
- **FII** (only Jun-Nov 2026 is on the server): injection days with FII buying, BANKNIFTY whole day 50% up, med -0.05% (n 12); with FII selling 45% up, med -0.09% (n 11). Small samples - the FII number is known only after the close.
- **Contrast - absorptions** (BANKNIFTY): window close -> close 53% up, med +0.02% (n 215); whole day 51% up, med +0.02% (n 344).

## 20 Nov 2026 (latest row) - checked against RBI's releases

- VRR inject: notice 2026-11-19 (release time not known), window 09:30-10:00, result release 10:20; notified Rs 50,000 cr, bids Rs 112,555 cr (cover 2.25), allotted Rs 50,003 cr, tenor 3 d (prids 64003 / 64007). BANKNIFTY from the 10:20 minute: +30 min +0.43%, +60 min +0.44%, to the close +2.45%; PSU BANK to the close +2.21%, PVT BANK +2.59%.
- VRR inject: notice 2026-11-20 (release 12:35), window 12:45-13:15, result release 13:40; notified Rs 25,000 cr, bids Rs 48,040 cr (cover 1.92), allotted Rs 25,007 cr, tenor 3 d (prids 64008 / 64010). BANKNIFTY from the 13:40 minute: +30 min +0.14%, +60 min +0.22%, to the close +0.36%; PSU BANK to the close +0.05%, PVT BANK +0.45%.
  - from the notice release minute 12:35: BANKNIFTY +30 min +0.16%, +60 min +0.09%, to the close +0.57%.
- Whole day 20 Nov: BANKNIFTY +2.49%, PSU BANK +1.23%, PVT BANK +3.01%, NIFTY +1.12%; FII net Rs 10,308 cr (local NSE provisional). BANKNIFTY's day beat 98% of all injection days since 2020 - an unusually strong injection day, not a typical one.

## How it was measured

- Operations: every RBI press release from Jan 2020 to 2026-11-20 whose title is a liquidity operation (VRR / VRRR notices + results, OMO purchase / sale / twist / G-SAP notices + results, FX swap notices + results, LTRO / TLTRO / SLTRO, 'measures to manage liquidity', policy statements for CRR) and the daily Money Market Operations tables.
- Direction: VRR, OMO purchase, FX buy/sell swap, LTRO/TLTRO and CRR cut = INJECT; VRRR, OMO sale, FX sell/buy swap and CRR hike = ABSORB; OMO twist (buy + sell on one day) = neutral.
- Size = amount allotted / accepted (Rs cr; notified when no result exists). Bid-cover = bids received / notified amount. Tight = cover >= 2x.
- Type: routine = VRR with tenor < 28 days; structural = OMO purchase (incl. G-SAP), FX buy/sell swap, LTRO/TLTRO, CRR cut. VRR with tenor >= 28 days is shown on its own line.
- Onset minute = the result time if GIR recorded it, else the auction window close; +30 / +60 min = index price 30 / 60 min after the onset; to the close = 15:30; next close = the next session's close, all relative to the price at the onset minute.
- Daily: day-of = previous close -> close of the auction day (contains the operation whatever its time); next session = close -> next close.
- Baselines: daily = every session in the window; minute = the same clock minute on every minute day with no injection.

## Tables

Cells: `% up, median %, best / worst, n`. Minute horizons start at the onset minute (window close, or the real result time where known) and use minute data (Nov 2023 ->): NIFTY BANK / NIFTY 50 = Kite index minutes; PSU / PVT BANK = Kite index minutes from 16 Oct 2026, a member proxy before (checked below). Daily horizons count each day once per row (several operations on one day share one daily move).

### NIFTY PSU BANK - minute horizons (from the onset minute)

## Data gaps (stated plainly)

- RBI month lists fetched: 83 of 83 months (2020_01 -> 2026_11).
- Liquidity press releases fetched: 4243; not fetched: 0; failed after 3 tries: 0.
- Times: RBI's press-release pages show the DATE only. Announcement and result times are known only where GIR's own feed recorded RBI's RSS time (v2/data/ext/regs.jsonl - 20 Nov 2026 only). Everywhere else the minute measures start at the AUCTION WINDOW CLOSE printed in the notice. On 20 Nov 2026 the results came 20 and 25 min after the window closed (09:30-10:00 -> 10:20; 12:45-13:15 -> 13:40). The rbidocs PDF copies sit behind a bot check and were not used.
- Operations with NO window time (day-only): CRR cut 4, CRR hike 3, OMO purchase 1, OMO purchase (G-SAP) 1, OMO twist (buy+sell) 2, VRR 13, VRRR 2.
- Notices with no matching result release: 2 (kept as rows, bids unknown).
- Minute data starts 2023-11-16 (Kite index minutes for NIFTY BANK / NIFTY 50; PSU / PVT BANK use a member proxy until 15 Oct 2026, real index minutes from 16 Oct 2026). Operations before that are measured on DAILY bars only.
- Daily bars: NIFTY BANK - Yahoo ^NSEBANK to 2021-11-14, Kite day candles from 2021-11-15; NIFTY PSU BANK - Yahoo ^CNXPSUBANK to 2021-11-14, Kite day candles from 2021-11-15; NIFTY PVT BANK - Yahoo member proxy (10 private banks, fixed weights, chained daily returns) 2019-11-01 -> 2021-11-12, Kite day candles from 2021-11-15; NIFTY 50 - Yahoo ^NSEI to 2021-11-14, Kite day candles from 2021-11-15.
- FII cash flows: only a short local history exists (/home/globalbot/panel_cache/fii_dii.json, NSE provisional, 99 days, 2026-06-11 -> 2026-11-20); the FII split covers only injections in that window. No earlier FII history was found under intraday/data or girdata.
- Money Market Operations daily tables parsed: 2353 (net liquidity found in 2348) - used for the deficit / surplus split.
- FX swap sizes are converted to Rs cr at Yahoo's USD/INR close of the auction day.
- CRR changes come from the policy statements (hand list in the script, each row cites its press release); time not known (day only).
- Overlaps: injections bunch in stress spells (Mar-Apr 2020) and in tight-liquidity spells (2024-26), and several operations can share one day; the daily rows count each day once per split.
- NIFTY PVT BANK daily before 15 Nov 2021 is a Yahoo member proxy; on the Kite overlap it matches the real index with daily-return correlation 0.987, same sign 94.7% of 1238 days.
